```csharp
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;
using Client.WebUiHost.Boot;
using Client.WebUiHost.Game.Actions;
using Client.WebUiHost.Game.Topics;

namespace Client.WebUiHost.Game
{
    public static class C4WebUiRegistration
    {
        public static void Register(WebSocketHub hub, ISkitCommands skitCommands)
        {
            // C4のpresentation topicとintent actionを同じ境界で登録する。skitの操作はGameUiと同じコマンドを通す
            // Register C4 presentation topics and intent actions at one boundary; skit operations go through the same commands as GameUi
            hub.RegisterTopic(GameStateTopic.TopicName, new GameStateTopic(hub));
            hub.RegisterTopic(TutorialPresentationTopic.TopicName, new TutorialPresentationTopic(hub));
            hub.RegisterTopic(WorldPinTopic.TopicName, new WorldPinTopic(hub));
            hub.RegisterTopic(SkitPresentationTopic.TopicName, new SkitPresentationTopic(hub));
            hub.RegisterAction(new TutorialAnchorAckAction());
            hub.RegisterAction(new SkitAdvanceActionHandler(skitCommands));
            hub.RegisterAction(new SkitSelectActionHandler(skitCommands));
            hub.RegisterAction(new SkitSetAutoActionHandler(skitCommands));
            hub.RegisterAction(new SkitSkipActionHandler(skitCommands));
            hub.RegisterAction(new SkitSetUiHiddenActionHandler(skitCommands));
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/State/WholeUiVisibility/WholeUiVisibilityModelTest.cs
using Client.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility;
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 全体を出すかが「Ctrl+U で隠していない かつ カットシーンでない」になることを確かめる（L0。Web App.tsx の !uiVisible || cutScene）
    /// Verifies that the whole UI shows exactly when it is not hidden by Ctrl+U and no cutscene plays (L0; Web App.tsx !uiVisible || cutScene)
    /// </summary>
    public class WholeUiVisibilityModelTest
    {
        private FakeWholeUiVisibilitySource _source;
        private WholeUiVisibilityModel _model;

        [SetUp]
        public void CreateModel()
        {
            _source = new FakeWholeUiVisibilitySource();
            _model = new WholeUiVisibilityModel(_source);
            _model.Initialize();
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void DisposeModel()
        {
            _model.Dispose();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 起動直後は全体を出す()
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(_model.IsShown.Value);
        }

        [Test]
        public void CtrlUで隠すと全体が隠れ戻すと出る()
        {
            _source.SetUiRootVisible(false);
            Assert.IsFalse(_model.IsShown.Value);
            _source.SetUiRootVisible(true);
            Assert.IsTrue(_model.IsShown.Value);
        }

        /// <web-test file="e2e/tests/phaseC4.spec.ts">cutscene state withdraws every web UI layer</web-test>
        [Test]
        public void カットシーンの間は全体が隠れ抜けると出る()
        {
            _source.SetCutScenePlaying(true);
            Assert.IsFalse(_model.IsShown.Value);
            _source.SetUiRootVisible(false);
            _source.SetCutScenePlaying(false);
            Assert.IsFalse(_model.IsShown.Value, "カットシーンを抜けても Ctrl+U で隠している間は出さない");
            _source.SetUiRootVisible(true);
            Assert.IsTrue(_model.IsShown.Value);
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/WholeUiVisibility/WholeUiRootHidingTest.cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Input;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Input;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Layers;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Stage;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 全体非表示がルート 1 つの display で全層（BehindStage〜FullScreenGate）を隠し、戻すと同じ要素のまま出ることを確かめる（L1）
    /// Verifies that whole-UI hiding hides every layer (BehindStage to FullScreenGate) through the root's display, and shows the same elements again (L1)
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// GameUiRoot は UIDocument を持つ MonoBehaviour で L1 では組めないため、GameUiRoot.SetWholeUiVisible の中身 UiLayerStack.SetAllLayersVisible をテストの rootVisualElement に組んで試す（D-28）。
    /// 隠れた要素は自身の resolvedStyle.display が Flex のままなので、隠れたかはルートの display と Pick で見る。
    /// GameUiRoot is a MonoBehaviour owning a UIDocument and cannot be built in L1, so the test builds UiLayerStack.SetAllLayersVisible, the body of GameUiRoot.SetWholeUiVisible, on the test rootVisualElement (D-28).
    /// Hidden elements keep their own resolvedStyle.display as Flex, so hiddenness is read from the root's display and Pick.
    /// </remarks>
    public class WholeUiRootHidingTest : ScreenTestHost
    {
        private readonly List<VisualElement> _probes = new();
        private UiLayerStack _stack;

        [SetUp]
        public void PutProbeOnEveryLayer()
        {
            // 全層の両族に 100px 角の印を置く
            // Place a 100px probe on both families of every layer
            _probes.Clear();
            _stack = UiLayerStack.Build(rootVisualElement);
            foreach (UiLayer layer in Enum.GetValues(typeof(UiLayer)))
            {
                foreach (UiFamily family in Enum.GetValues(typeof(UiFamily)))
                {
                    var probe = new VisualElement { name = $"probe-{layer}-{family}" };
                    probe.style.position = Position.Absolute;
                    probe.style.left = 0;
                    probe.style.top = 0;
                    probe.style.width = 100;
                    probe.style.height = 100;
                    _stack.GetLayer(layer, family).Add(probe);
                    _probes.Add(probe);
                }
            }
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void ShowRootAndClearFocusFlag()
        {
            // 基底はルートのインラインの指定をテスト間で消さないので、隠したままにしない
            // The base keeps the root's inline style across tests, so never leave it hidden
            _stack.SetAllLayersVisible(true);
            UiInputExclusivity.SetGameUiTextInputFocused(false);
        }

        /// <web-test file="e2e/tests/system/commonHud.spec.ts">ui.visibility=falseでPortalを含む全UIを退避し復帰する</web-test>
        /// <web-test file="e2e/tests/phaseC4.spec.ts">cutscene state withdraws every web UI layer</web-test>
        [Test]
        public void 隠すと全層の要素が拾えず戻すと同じ要素が前面から拾える()
        {
            var topmost = _probes[_probes.Count - 1];
            Assert.AreSame(topmost, rootVisualElement.panel.Pick(new Vector2(50, 50)));

            _stack.SetAllLayersVisible(false);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.None, rootVisualElement.resolvedStyle.display);
            var picked = rootVisualElement.panel.Pick(new Vector2(50, 50));
            Assert.IsFalse(picked != null && rootVisualElement.Contains(picked), $"隠している間に {picked?.name} が拾えた");

            _stack.SetAllLayersVisible(true);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.Flex, rootVisualElement.resolvedStyle.display);
            Assert.AreSame(topmost, rootVisualElement.panel.Pick(new Vector2(50, 50)));
            foreach (var probe in _probes) Assert.IsNotNull(probe.panel, $"{probe.name} が階層から外れた");
        }

        [Test]
        public void 隠している間も層の中の要素の表示指定は変えない()
        {
            _stack.SetAllLayersVisible(false);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            foreach (var probe in _probes) Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.Flex, probe.resolvedStyle.display, $"{probe.name} の display を書き換えた");
        }

        [Test]
        public void 隠す前に入力欄のフォーカスを外しキーの抑止を残さない()
        {
            // 入力規則を付けたモーダルの層の入力欄にフォーカスを置く（不具合報告の入力中にカットシーンへ入る形）
            // Focus a text field on the modal layer carrying the input rules (as when a cutscene starts while typing a bug report)
            var modal = _stack.GetLayer(UiLayer.Modal, UiFamily.Stage);
            GameUiInputRules.Attach(modal);
            var textField = new TextField { name = "hiding-text" };
            textField.style.width = 300;
            modal.Add(textField);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            textField.Focus();
            Assert.IsTrue(UiInputExclusivity.IsTextInputFocused);

            _stack.SetAllLayersVisible(false);

            Assert.IsNull(rootVisualElement.panel.focusController.focusedElement, "隠した後も入力欄がフォーカスを持っている");
            Assert.IsFalse(UiInputExclusivity.IsTextInputFocused, "隠している間もゲームのキーが抑止されたまま");
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui.State/WholeUiVisibility/IWholeUiVisibilitySource.cs
using System;

namespace Client.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 全体非表示の 2 つの源。どちらも購読時に今の値を流す
    /// The two sources of whole-UI hiding; both replay the current value on subscribe
    /// </summary>
    public interface IWholeUiVisibilitySource
    {
        IObservable<bool> ObserveUiRootVisible();
        IObservable<bool> ObserveCutScenePlaying();
    }
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui.State/WholeUiVisibility/GameWholeUiVisibilitySource.cs
using System;
using Client.Game.Common;
using Client.Game.InGame.UI.UIState;
using UniRx;

namespace Client.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ゲームの Ctrl+U（UIRoot）とカットシーン（GameStateController）を、全体非表示の源として読む（Web の ui.visibility と game_state）
    /// Reads the game's Ctrl+U (UIRoot) and cutscene (GameStateController) as the whole-UI hiding sources (Web ui.visibility and game_state)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class GameWholeUiVisibilitySource : IWholeUiVisibilitySource
    {
        private readonly UIRoot _uiRoot;

        public GameWholeUiVisibilitySource(UIRoot uiRoot)
        {
            _uiRoot = uiRoot;
        }

        public IObservable<bool> ObserveUiRootVisible()
        {
            return _uiRoot.OnVisibilityChanged;
        }

        // 状態の通知は変化だけなので、購読の時点の状態を先に流す
        // The state stream carries changes only, so emit the state at subscription first
        public IObservable<bool> ObserveCutScenePlaying()
        {
            return Observable.Defer(() => GameStateController.OnStateChanged.Select(IsCutScene).StartWith(IsCutScene(GameStateController.CurrentState)));

            #region Internal

            static bool IsCutScene(GameStateType state)
            {
                return state == GameStateType.CutScene;
            }

            #endregion
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui.State/WholeUiVisibility/WholeUiVisibilityModel.cs
using System;
using UniRx;
using VContainer.Unity;

namespace Client.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// UITK 全体を出すか。Web App.tsx の「uiVisible かつ カットシーンでない」（A-80）
    /// Whether the whole UITK UI shows; the Web App.tsx "uiVisible and not a cutscene" (A-80)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class WholeUiVisibilityModel : IInitializable, IDisposable
    {
        private readonly IWholeUiVisibilitySource _source;
        private readonly ReactiveProperty<bool> _isShown = new(true);
        private IDisposable _subscription;

        public IReadOnlyReactiveProperty<bool> IsShown => _isShown;

        public WholeUiVisibilityModel(IWholeUiVisibilitySource source)
        {
            _source = source;
        }

        public void Initialize()
        {
            _subscription = _source.ObserveUiRootVisible()
                .CombineLatest(_source.ObserveCutScenePlaying(), (visible, cutScene) => visible && !cutScene)
                .Subscribe(shown => _isShown.Value = shown);
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            _subscription?.Dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

（`GameStateType` と `GameStateController` の名前空間は `grep -n "namespace\|enum GameStateType" moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Game/Common/GameStateController.cs` で確かめる。この計画は `Client.Game.Common` で書いた）

- [ ] **Step 3: ルートの持ち主に口を足し、Presenter を書く**

`S/Client.Ui/Foundation/Layers/UiLayerStack.cs` をこの形にする（ルートを保持し、末尾へ `SetAllLayersVisible` を足す。既存の `Build`・`GetLayer` の中身は変えない。D-28）:

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Foundation/Layers/UiLayerStack.cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Stage;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Foundation.Layers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 層コンテナをenum順に並べ層と族で引く
    /// Layer containers in enum order, looked up by layer and family
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class UiLayerStack
    {
        private const string LayerUssClassName = "game-ui-layer";

        private readonly VisualElement _root;
        private readonly Dictionary<UiLayer, UiStageFrame> _frames;

        private UiLayerStack(VisualElement root, Dictionary<UiLayer, UiStageFrame> frames)
        {
            _root = root;
            _frames = frames;
        }

        public static UiLayerStack Build(VisualElement root)
        {
            // ルートも層もクリックを受けず、空の場所はワールドへ抜ける
            // Neither the root nor the layers take clicks, so empty spots fall through to the world
            root.pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            var frames = new Dictionary<UiLayer, UiStageFrame>();

            // 後に Add した子ほど前面に描かれるので enum 順に足す
            // Later children draw in front, so add them in enum order
            foreach (UiLayer layer in Enum.GetValues(typeof(UiLayer)))
            {
                var container = CreateLayerContainer(layer);
                root.Add(container);
                frames.Add(layer, UiStageFrame.Build(container));
            }

            return new UiLayerStack(root, frames);

            #region Internal

            VisualElement CreateLayerContainer(UiLayer layer)
            {
                var container = new VisualElement { name = $"layer-{layer}", pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore };
                // 配置はUSSが持つ
                // Placement lives in USS
                container.AddToClassList(LayerUssClassName);
                return container;
            }

            #endregion
        }

        public VisualElement GetLayer(UiLayer layer, UiFamily family)
        {
            return _frames[layer].Get(family);
        }

        // 全部隠し（HideAll）の中身。全層をルート 1 つの display でまとめて出し入れする。要素は階層に残すので購読・入力中の値・動きは保たれる（A-80）
        // Body of HideAll: shows or hides every layer at once through the root's display; elements stay in the hierarchy, so subscriptions, typed values and motion survive (A-80)
        public void SetAllLayersVisible(bool visible)
        {
            if (visible)
            {
                // 戻すときはインラインの指定を外し、USS の既定へ返す
                // When showing again, drop the inline value and return to the USS default
                _root.style.display = StyleKeyword.Null;
                return;
            }

            // 隠れた入力欄がフォーカスを持ち続けるとゲームのキー抑止が残るので先に外す（Web の focus fixup と同じ。フォーカス無しは外すものが無い）
            // A hidden text field keeping focus would leave the game's key suppression on, so blur first (same as the Web focus fixup; no focus means nothing to blur)
            _root.focusController.focusedElement?.Blur();
            _root.style.display = DisplayStyle.None;
        }
    }
}
```

`S/Client.Ui/Foundation/Root/GameUiRoot.cs` の末尾（`SetRootClassEnabled` の次）へメソッド 1 本を足す。前例は同じクラスの `SetRootClassEnabled`（ルート全体の状態は持ち主の Set で書く）:

```csharp
        // Whole-UI hiding (HideAll) for Ctrl+U and cutscenes; shows or hides every layer, BehindStage notifications included, through the single root display (A-80)
        public void SetWholeUiVisible(bool visible)
```

```csharp
using System;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Root;
using Client.Ui.State.WholeUiVisibility;
using UniRx;
using VContainer.Unity;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.WholeUiVisibility
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Ctrl+U とカットシーンを合わせた値で GameUi 全体を出し入れする（全部隠し・HideAll）。隠し方はルートの持ち主 GameUiRoot が持つ（Web App.tsx:86。D-28）
    /// Shows or hides the whole GameUi (HideAll) from the combined Ctrl+U and cutscene value; the root owner GameUiRoot owns how to hide (Web App.tsx:86; D-28)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class WholeUiVisibilityPresenter : IInitializable, IDisposable
    {
        private readonly GameUiRoot _gameUiRoot;
        private readonly WholeUiVisibilityModel _model;
        private IDisposable _subscription;

        public WholeUiVisibilityPresenter(GameUiRoot gameUiRoot, WholeUiVisibilityModel model)
        {
            _gameUiRoot = gameUiRoot;
            _model = model;
        }

        public void Initialize()
        {
            _subscription = _model.IsShown.Subscribe(_gameUiRoot.SetWholeUiVisible);
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            _subscription?.Dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/SkitTestData.cs
using System;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    /// <summary>
    /// スキットの L1 の提示。Web の mock（話者 Moore・session blocking-1・Route A/B）と同じ値を本物の生成から作る

    {
        public const string SessionId = "blocking-1";
        public static readonly string[] TextIntents = { "advance", "set-auto", "skip", "set-ui-hidden" };
        public static readonly string[] ChoiceIntents = { "select", "set-auto", "skip", "set-ui-hidden" };

        public static SkitViewModel Text(string sessionId, int revision, string body, string[] intents)
        {
            return SkitViewModel.From(SkitPresentationData.CreateBlocking(sessionId, revision, "Moore", body, Array.Empty<SkitChoice>(), true, false, false, false, false, "typewriter", 50, intents));
        }

        public static SkitViewModel Choices(int revision, string[] intents)
        {
            var choices = new[] { new SkitChoice { ChoiceId = "route-a", Label = "Route A" }, new SkitChoice { ChoiceId = "route-b", Label = "Route B" } };
            return SkitViewModel.From(SkitPresentationData.CreateBlocking(SessionId, revision, "Moore", "Blocking message", choices, true, false, false, false, false, "instant", 0, intents));
        }

        public static SkitViewModel Controls(int revision, bool textAreaVisible, bool transitionVisible, bool autoEnabled, bool skipActive, bool uiHidden, string[] intents)
        {
            return SkitViewModel.From(SkitPresentationData.CreateBlocking(SessionId, revision, "Moore", "Blocking message", Array.Empty<SkitChoice>(), textAreaVisible, transitionVisible, autoEnabled, skipActive, uiHidden, "instant", 0, intents));
        }

        public static SkitViewModel Background(int revision, string speaker, string body)
        {
            return SkitViewModel.From(SkitPresentationData.CreateBackground("background-1", revision, speaker, body));
        }

        public static SkitViewModel None(int revision)
        {
            return SkitViewModel.From(SkitPresentationData.CreateNone(SessionId, revision));
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/RecordingSkitCommands.cs
using Client.Tests.Ui.TestDoubles;
using Client.Ui.State.Commands;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    public enum SkitCommandKind
    {
        Advance,
        Select,
        SetAuto,
        Skip,
        SetUiHidden,
    }

    // 発行された会話の操作 1 件。choiceId と真偽は該当する操作だけが持つ
    // One issued skit operation; the choice id and the flag are set only by the operations that carry them
    public readonly struct SkitCommandRecord
    {
        public readonly SkitCommandKind Kind;
        public readonly string SessionId;
        public readonly int SceneRevision;
        public readonly string ChoiceId;
        public readonly bool Flag;

        public SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind kind, string sessionId, int sceneRevision, string choiceId, bool flag)
        {
            Kind = kind;
            SessionId = sessionId;
            SceneRevision = sceneRevision;
            ChoiceId = choiceId;
            Flag = flag;
        }

        public override string ToString() => $"{Kind}({SessionId}, {SceneRevision}, {ChoiceId}, {Flag})";
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 会話の操作の記録係。どれも受理を返す
    /// Recorder of skit operations; every call returns accepted
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class RecordingSkitCommands : CommandRecorder<SkitCommandRecord>, ISkitCommands
    {
        public UiCommandResult Advance(string sessionId, int sceneRevision) => Accept(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Advance, sessionId, sceneRevision, null, false));
        public UiCommandResult Select(string sessionId, int sceneRevision, string choiceId) => Accept(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Select, sessionId, sceneRevision, choiceId, false));
        public UiCommandResult SetAuto(string sessionId, int sceneRevision, bool enabled) => Accept(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.SetAuto, sessionId, sceneRevision, null, enabled));
        public UiCommandResult Skip(string sessionId, int sceneRevision) => Accept(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Skip, sessionId, sceneRevision, null, false));
        public UiCommandResult SetUiHidden(string sessionId, int sceneRevision, bool hidden) => Accept(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.SetUiHidden, sessionId, sceneRevision, null, hidden));

        private UiCommandResult Accept(SkitCommandRecord record)
        {
            Record(record);
            return UiCommandAccepted.Instance;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/SkitToolbarViewTest.cs
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Toolbar;
using NUnit.Framework;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.Components.ComponentStyleAssert;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ツールバーの 4 つのボタンが session と版を付けて操作を送り、許可とスキップ中で押せなくなることを確かめる（L1。Web SkitToolbar.tsx）
    /// Verifies that the four toolbar buttons send operations with session and revision, and become unpressable without allowance or while skipping (L1; Web SkitToolbar.tsx)
    /// </summary>
    public class SkitToolbarViewTest : ScreenTestHost
    {
        private RecordingSkitCommands _commands;
        private SkitToolbar _toolbar;

        [SetUp]
        public void MountToolbar()
        {
            _commands = new RecordingSkitCommands();
            _toolbar = Mount(new SkitToolbar(_commands, Toaster));
        }

        [Test]
        public void ツールは版を付けて自動スキップUI非表示を送り自動ONはシアンの印を持つ()
        {
            _toolbar.Render(SkitTestData.Controls(4, true, false, true, false, false, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsTrue(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-auto").ClassListContains(SkitToolbar.ActiveToolUssClassName));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-show-ui")));

            // 画面 USS の上書きが IconButton の既定（--text-muted）に勝つ: 自動 ON はシアン、他は高コントラスト
            // The screen USS override beats IconButton's default (--text-muted): auto-on is cyan, the rest high contrast
            AssertColor(Rgba(0, 221, 255, 1f), GlyphColor("skit-tool-auto"), "--select-cyan");
            AssertColor(Hex("#FFFFFF"), GlyphColor("skit-tool-skip"), "--text-high-contrast");

            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-auto"));
            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-skip"));
            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-hide-ui"));

            Assert.AreEqual(3, _commands.Issued.Count);
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.SetAuto, SkitTestData.SessionId, 4, null, false), _commands.Issued[0], "自動 ON から押すと OFF を送る");
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Skip, SkitTestData.SessionId, 4, null, false), _commands.Issued[1]);
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.SetUiHidden, SkitTestData.SessionId, 4, null, true), _commands.Issued[2]);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 許可の無い操作とスキップ中のスキップは押せない()
        {
            _toolbar.Render(SkitTestData.Controls(5, true, false, false, true, false, new[] { "skip" }));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-auto").enabledSelf);
            Assert.IsFalse(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-skip").enabledSelf, "スキップ中のスキップ");
            Assert.IsFalse(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-hide-ui").enabledSelf);
            AssertColor(Hex("#A6A7AB"), GlyphColor("skit-tool-auto"), "無効は --text-muted");

            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-auto"));
            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-skip"));
            Assert.AreEqual(0, _commands.Issued.Count);
        }

        [Test]
        public void UI非表示の間は復帰だけを出し押すと表示へ戻す()
        {
            _toolbar.Render(SkitTestData.Controls(6, true, false, false, false, true, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-auto")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-skip")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-tool-hide-ui")));
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-show-ui")));

            simulate.Click(Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, "skit-show-ui"));
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.SetUiHidden, SkitTestData.SessionId, 6, null, false), _commands.Issued[0]);
        }

        private Color GlyphColor(string testid)
        {
            var glyph = Find<IconButton>(_toolbar, testid).Q<SkitToolGlyph>();
            Assert.IsTrue(glyph.customStyle.TryGetValue(new CustomStyleProperty<Color>(IconButton.GlyphColorProperty), out var color), testid);
            return color;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolGlyphShape.cs
namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Toolbar
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ツールの図形の種類（Web features/skit/icons.tsx の AutoIcon・SkipIcon・HideUiIcon・ShowUiIcon）
    /// Kinds of tool glyph (Web features/skit/icons.tsx AutoIcon, SkipIcon, HideUiIcon, ShowUiIcon)
    /// </summary>
    public enum SkitToolGlyphShape
    {
        Auto,
        Skip,
        HideUi,
        ShowUi,
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolGlyphPaths.cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Toolbar
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Web の SVG（viewBox 24）の path を Painter2D の線へ写す。座標は Web の d 属性のまま
    /// Copies the Web SVG paths (viewBox 24) onto Painter2D strokes; coordinates are the Web d attributes as is
    /// </summary>
    public static class SkitToolGlyphPaths
    {
        public const float ViewBoxSize = 24f;

        public static void Stroke(Painter2D painter, SkitToolGlyphShape shape, float scale, Vector2 offset)
        {
            switch (shape)
            {
                case SkitToolGlyphShape.Auto:
                    StrokeCircle(12f, 12f, 9f);
                    StrokePolyline(true, 10f, 8f, 16f, 12f, 10f, 16f);
                    return;
                case SkitToolGlyphShape.Skip:
                    StrokePolyline(false, 4f, 6f, 10f, 12f, 4f, 18f);
                    StrokePolyline(false, 11f, 6f, 17f, 12f, 11f, 18f);
                    StrokePolyline(false, 20f, 6f, 20f, 18f);
                    return;
                case SkitToolGlyphShape.HideUi:
                    StrokeEye();
                    StrokeCircle(12f, 12f, 3f);
                    StrokePolyline(false, 4f, 4f, 20f, 20f);
                    return;
                case SkitToolGlyphShape.ShowUi:
                    StrokeEye();
                    StrokeCircle(12f, 12f, 3f);
                    return;
                default:
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(shape), shape, null);
            }

            #region Internal

            Vector2 At(float x, float y)
            {
                return new Vector2(x * scale, y * scale) + offset;
            }

            void StrokeCircle(float centerX, float centerY, float radius)
            {
                painter.BeginPath();
                painter.Arc(At(centerX, centerY), radius * scale, Angle.Degrees(0f), Angle.Degrees(360f));
                painter.Stroke();
            }

            // 座標の組を順に結ぶ。closed なら始点へ戻して閉じる（SVG の Z）
            // Joins coordinate pairs in order; when closed, return to the start (SVG Z)
            void StrokePolyline(bool closed, params float[] points)
            {
                painter.BeginPath();
                painter.MoveTo(At(points[0], points[1]));
                for (var i = 2; i < points.Length; i += 2) painter.LineTo(At(points[i], points[i + 1]));
                if (closed) painter.ClosePath();
                painter.Stroke();
            }

            // M2.5 12 C6 6.5 9 5 12 5 C15 5 18 6.5 21.5 12 C18 17.5 15 19 12 19 C9 19 6 17.5 2.5 12 Z
            void StrokeEye()
            {
                painter.BeginPath();
                painter.MoveTo(At(2.5f, 12f));
                painter.BezierCurveTo(At(6f, 6.5f), At(9f, 5f), At(12f, 5f));
                painter.BezierCurveTo(At(15f, 5f), At(18f, 6.5f), At(21.5f, 12f));
                painter.BezierCurveTo(At(18f, 17.5f), At(15f, 19f), At(12f, 19f));
                painter.BezierCurveTo(At(9f, 19f), At(6f, 17.5f), At(2.5f, 12f));
                painter.ClosePath();
                painter.Stroke();
            }

            #endregion
        }
    }
}
```

（着手時に `grep -n "d=\|<circle\|<path\|<line" moorestech_web/webui/src/features/skit/icons.tsx` で座標を突き合わせ、違えば Web に合わせる）

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolGlyph.cs
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Theme;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Toolbar
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ツールの図形を、影を下へずらした写しの上に本体を重ねて描く（Web の drop-shadow(0 1px 1px) のぼかし無しの近似。LX-52）
    /// Draws a tool glyph over a copy shifted down as its shadow (a blur-less approximation of the Web drop-shadow(0 1px 1px); LX-52)
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// 色と線幅は IconButton のつまみ（GlyphColorProperty・GlyphStrokeProperty）を読み、状態ごとの色は画面 USS が上書きする。影だけは IconButton に口が無いので自分の --skit-tool-glyph-shadow-* を読む（D-14・D-27）
    /// Colour and stroke come from IconButton's knobs (GlyphColorProperty, GlyphStrokeProperty), overridden per state by the screen USS; only the shadow reads this glyph's own --skit-tool-glyph-shadow-*, as IconButton has no entry for it (D-14, D-27)
    /// </remarks>
    public sealed class SkitToolGlyph : VisualElement
    {
        public const string UssClassName = "skit-tool-glyph";

        private readonly SkitToolGlyphShape _shape;
        private Color _color;
        private float _stroke;
        private Color _shadowColor;
        private float _shadowOffset;
        private bool _hasTokens;

        public SkitToolGlyph(SkitToolGlyphShape shape)
        {
            _shape = shape;
            AddToClassList(UssClassName);
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            generateVisualContent += Draw;
            RegisterCallback<CustomStyleResolvedEvent>(_ => OnCustomStyleResolved());
        }

        private void OnCustomStyleResolved()
        {
            // ホバー・無効・自動 ON のたびに解決し直されるので、読み直して描き直す（線幅は 24 単位の無次元の数）
            // Re-resolved on hover, disable and auto-on, so re-read and redraw (the stroke is a unitless number in 24 units)
            var hasColor = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, IconButton.GlyphColorProperty, out _color);
            var hasStroke = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadNumber(customStyle, IconButton.GlyphStrokeProperty, out _stroke);
            var hasShadow = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--skit-tool-glyph-shadow-color", out _shadowColor);
            var hasOffset = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--skit-tool-glyph-shadow-offset", out _shadowOffset);
            _hasTokens = hasColor && hasStroke && hasShadow && hasOffset;
            MarkDirtyRepaint();
        }

        private void Draw(MeshGenerationContext context)
        {
            // silent-ok: トークンが読めない間は描かない / UssCustomStyleReader が読めなかったトークン名を LogError で出す
            if (!_hasTokens) return;

            // 24 単位の座標を要素の幅へ合わせ、線幅も同じ倍率で太らせる（SVG の stroke-width と同じ）
            // Fit the 24-unit coordinates to the element width and scale the stroke by the same factor (same as SVG stroke-width)
            var scale = contentRect.width / SkitToolGlyphPaths.ViewBoxSize;
            var painter = context.painter2D;
            painter.lineWidth = _stroke * scale;
            painter.lineCap = LineCap.Round;
            painter.lineJoin = LineJoin.Round;

            // 影を先に、本体をその上に描く
            // Draw the shadow first and the glyph on top
            painter.strokeColor = _shadowColor;
            SkitToolGlyphPaths.Stroke(painter, _shape, scale, new Vector2(0f, _shadowOffset));
            painter.strokeColor = _color;
            SkitToolGlyphPaths.Stroke(painter, _shape, scale, Vector2.zero);
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolbar.cs
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using Client.Ui.State.Commands;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;
using Client.Ui.State.Toast;
using UniRx;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Toolbar
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 実画面の右上に並ぶ自動・スキップ・UI 非表示と、UI 非表示中だけの復帰ボタン（Web SkitToolbar.tsx の SkitToolbar・SkitRestoreButton）
    /// Auto, skip and hide-UI in the screen's top-right, plus the restore button shown only while the UI is hidden (Web SkitToolbar.tsx SkitToolbar and SkitRestoreButton)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitToolbar : VisualElement
    {
        public const string ActiveToolUssClassName = "skit-toolbar__tool--active";
        private const string ToolUssClassName = "skit-toolbar__tool";
        private const string LeadToolUssClassName = "skit-toolbar__tool--lead";

        private readonly ISkitCommands _commands;
        private readonly UiCommandResultToaster _toaster;
        private readonly IconButton _auto;
        private readonly IconButton _skip;
        private readonly IconButton _hideUi;
        private readonly IconButton _showUi;
        private SkitViewModel _model = SkitViewModel.Empty;

        public SkitToolbar(ISkitCommands commands, UiCommandResultToaster toaster)
        {
            _commands = commands;
            _toaster = toaster;
            AddToClassList("skit-toolbar");
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;

            // 自動送りの ON/OFF は同じ図形の色替えで示し、図形は差し替えない
            // Auto-advance on/off is a color swap on one glyph; the glyph is never replaced
            _auto = AddTool("skit-tool-auto", SkitToolGlyphShape.Auto, true);
            _skip = AddTool("skit-tool-skip", SkitToolGlyphShape.Skip, false);
            _hideUi = AddTool("skit-tool-hide-ui", SkitToolGlyphShape.HideUi, false);
            _showUi = AddTool("skit-show-ui", SkitToolGlyphShape.ShowUi, true);

            // 押した時点の session と版を付けて送る
            // Send with the session and revision at the moment of the press
            _auto.OnClicked.Subscribe(_ => Report("skit.set_auto", _commands.SetAuto(_model.SessionId, _model.SceneRevision, !_model.AutoEnabled)));
            _skip.OnClicked.Subscribe(_ => Report("skit.skip", _commands.Skip(_model.SessionId, _model.SceneRevision)));
            _hideUi.OnClicked.Subscribe(_ => Report("skit.set_ui_hidden", _commands.SetUiHidden(_model.SessionId, _model.SceneRevision, true)));
            _showUi.OnClicked.Subscribe(_ => Report("skit.set_ui_hidden", _commands.SetUiHidden(_model.SessionId, _model.SceneRevision, false)));
        }

        public void Render(SkitViewModel model)
        {
            // 3 つのツールは窓かツールだけの面で出し、復帰は UI 非表示の面でだけ出す
            // The three tools show on the window and toolbar-only surfaces; restore shows only on the hidden-UI surface
            _model = model;
            var controlsShown = model.Surface == SkitSurface.Window || model.Surface == SkitSurface.ToolbarOnly;
            SetShown(_auto, controlsShown);
            SetShown(_skip, controlsShown);
            SetShown(_hideUi, controlsShown);
            SetShown(_showUi, model.Surface == SkitSurface.RestoreOnly);

            // 押せるかは許可から決め、自動送り ON だけシアンで灯す
            // Pressability follows the allowance, and only auto-advance ON lights cyan
            _auto.SetEnabled(model.CanSetAuto);
            _skip.SetEnabled(model.CanSkip);
            _hideUi.SetEnabled(model.CanSetUiHidden);
            _auto.EnableInClassList(ActiveToolUssClassName, model.AutoEnabled);
        }

        // ポーズ中は 4 つとも拾わず、背後のポーズへも抜けない位置に表示だけ残す（Web の .inputBlocked）
        // While paused none of the four pick, leaving only the display (Web .inputBlocked)
        public void SetInteractive(bool interactive)
        {
            var mode = interactive ? PickingMode.Position : PickingMode.Ignore;
            _auto.pickingMode = mode;
            _skip.pickingMode = mode;
            _hideUi.pickingMode = mode;
            _showUi.pickingMode = mode;
        }

        private IconButton AddTool(string testid, SkitToolGlyphShape shape, bool lead)
        {
            var button = new IconButton(testid, new SkitToolGlyph(shape));
            button.AddToClassList(ToolUssClassName);
            button.EnableInClassList(LeadToolUssClassName, lead);
            Add(button);
            return button;
        }

        private void Report(string commandName, UiCommandResult result)
        {
            _toaster.Report(commandName, result);
        }

        private static void SetShown(VisualElement element, bool shown)
        {
            element.style.display = shown ? DisplayStyle.Flex : DisplayStyle.None;
        }
    }
}
```

（復帰ボタンは 3 つのツールと同時には出ないので、先頭の印 `--lead` を自分にも付けて左の間隔を 0 にする）

- [ ] **Step 4: USS を書いて tss へ足す**

```css
/* S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolbar.uss */
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/features/skit/controls/SkitToolbar.module.css の .toolbar・.toolButton と skitTokens.css の --skit-tool-icon-shadow */
/* 実画面の右上に並ぶ面を持たないツール。IconButton の寸法をボタン枠と同寸へ局所上書きし、図形の色は状態ごとに IconButton の図形のつまみで渡す */
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/features/skit/controls/SkitToolbar.module.css .toolbar and .toolButton, plus --skit-tool-icon-shadow in skitTokens.css */
/* Faceless tools in the screen's top-right; IconButton's size is locally overridden to the frame size and the glyph color is passed per state through IconButton's glyph knob */

.skit-toolbar {
    position: absolute;
    top: var(--skit-toolbar-inset-top);
    right: var(--skit-toolbar-inset-right);
    flex-direction: row;
}

/* 既定は Tokens.uss の :root にあるので、要素自身に宣言すれば継承より勝つ（--icon-button-icon-size が効くのは既定の × だけで、図形の寸法は下の .skit-tool-glyph が持つ。Web と同じ上書きを写す） */
/* The defaults live on :root in Tokens.uss, so declaring on the element beats inheritance (--icon-button-icon-size only sizes the default cross; the glyph size is set on .skit-tool-glyph below; mirrors the Web override) */
.skit-toolbar .skit-toolbar__tool {
    --icon-button-size: var(--skit-tool-button-size);
    --icon-button-icon-size: var(--skit-tool-button-size);
    margin-left: var(--skit-tool-gap);
    opacity: var(--skit-tool-icon-opacity);
}

.skit-toolbar .skit-toolbar__tool--lead {
    margin-left: 0;
}

.skit-toolbar .skit-tool-glyph {
    --skit-tool-glyph-shadow-color: var(--skit-window-face);
    --skit-tool-glyph-shadow-offset: 1px;
    width: var(--skit-tool-button-size);
    height: var(--skit-tool-button-size);
}

/* 通常の色と線幅。IconButton の .icon-button .icon-button__glyph（2 クラス）へ 3 クラスで勝つ。有効時のホバーは IconButton の 4 つ（:hover:enabled）が --text-high-contrast にし、自動 ON の上でも白になる（Web の :not(:disabled):hover が [data-enabled] より強いのと同じ） */
/* Normal colour and stroke; three classes beat IconButton's two-class .icon-button .icon-button__glyph. Enabled hover is IconButton's four-part :hover:enabled rule (--text-high-contrast), which also wins over auto-on, as the Web :not(:disabled):hover beats [data-enabled] */
.skit-toolbar .skit-toolbar__tool .skit-tool-glyph {
    --icon-button-glyph-color: var(--text-high-contrast);
    --icon-button-glyph-stroke: var(--skit-tool-icon-stroke-width);
}

/* 自動送り ON だけシアンで灯す（同じ 3 つで後に置く） */
/* Only auto-advance ON lights cyan (same three parts, placed later) */
.skit-toolbar .skit-toolbar__tool--active .skit-tool-glyph {
    --icon-button-glyph-color: var(--select-cyan);
}

/* 押せないツールは従属テキストへ減衰させる。4 つで自動 ON にも勝つ（Web の :disabled が後に来て勝つのと同じ） */
/* Unpressable tools fade to secondary text; four parts beat auto-on too (as the later Web :disabled wins) */
.skit-toolbar .skit-toolbar__tool:disabled .skit-tool-glyph {
    --icon-button-glyph-color: var(--text-muted);
}
```

`--skit-tool-glyph-shadow-offset: 1px` は Web の `--skit-tool-icon-shadow: 0 1px 1px var(--skit-window-face)` の縦のずれ。`TokenConversionExclusions.txt` が「部品の段でメッシュで描く」として除外した変数で、色リテラルではないので画面 USS に置く（`UiStyleConventionTest` が数値の直書きを咎めたら、`DerivedTokens.uss` の末尾へ `--skit-tool-icon-shadow-offset: 1px;` を足してそれを参照する）。`SetEnabled(false)` で `:disabled` が付き `:enabled` が外れることは C2-b の `IconButtonTest`（無効ではホバーしても持ち上げない）が確かめ済み。

`GameUiTheme.tss` の `/* == section: Screens == */` 区画の末尾へ 1 行:
```css
@import url("project://database/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Toolbar/SkitToolbar.uss");
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/SkitChoiceListTest.cs
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Choices;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 選択肢が choiceId で select を送り、会話窓の上の右端から下へ積まれることを確かめる（L1。Web SkitChoiceList）
    /// Verifies that choices send select by choiceId and stack upward from the right edge just above the window (L1; Web SkitChoiceList)
    /// </summary>
    public class SkitChoiceListTest : ScreenTestHost
    {
        private RecordingSkitCommands _commands;
        private SkitChoiceList _choices;

        [SetUp]
        public void MountChoices()
        {
            _commands = new RecordingSkitCommands();
            _choices = Mount(new SkitChoiceList(_commands, Toaster));
            _choices.Render(SkitTestData.Choices(7, SkitTestData.ChoiceIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 選択肢を押すとそのchoiceIdで選ぶ()
        {
            simulate.Click(Find<Button>(_choices, "skit-choice-route-b"));
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Select, SkitTestData.SessionId, 7, "route-b", false), _commands.Issued[0]);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 選択肢は窓の上へ右端から下から積む()
        {
            // 窓の高さ 204px＋0.7px の上、右 5.3px。最初の選択肢が一番下で、間隔は 14.7px
            // Above the 204px window plus 0.7px, 5.3px from the right; the first choice is lowest, 14.7px apart
            var routeA = Find<Button>(_choices, "skit-choice-route-a").worldBound;
            var routeB = Find<Button>(_choices, "skit-choice-route-b").worldBound;
            Assert.AreEqual(164f, routeA.width, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(30.7f, routeA.height, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(1280f - 5.3f, routeA.xMax, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(720f - 204f - 0.7f, routeA.yMax, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(routeA.yMin - 14.7f, routeB.yMax, 0.5f);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 板の種別を部品へ足し、菱形と選択肢 1 つを書く**

選択肢の板の面は、部品 `GamePanelSurface` の新しい種別で描く。画面から internal の `GamePanelFace` を new しない（D-37）。

`S/Client.Ui/Components/Panels/GamePanelSurface/GamePanelSurfaceKind.cs` の列挙の末尾（`Skit,` の次）へ 1 値を足し、要約の「（hud・skit）」を「（hud・skit・skit-choice）」に直す:

```csharp
        SkitChoice,
```

`GamePanelSurface.cs` の `KindClassName` の switch の `GamePanelSurfaceKind.Skit` の腕の次へ 1 本を足す:

```csharp
                    GamePanelSurfaceKind.SkitChoice => "game-panel-surface--skit-choice",
```

`GamePanelSurface.uss` の末尾へ足す（`--skit-choice-edge-fade` は既存の `SkitTokens.uss`、`--skit-choice-active-face` はこの Task の Step 4 で `DerivedTokens.uss` に足す。既存の skit の種類も `--skit-window-*` を直に引く前例）:

```css
/* skit-choice: スキットの選択肢の板。持ち主のボタンいっぱいに敷き、左右だけを水平フェードさせ、押している間は面をシアン寄りにする（Web SkitChoiceList.module.css の .choice::before と .choice:active::before） */
/* skit-choice: plate under a skit choice button; fills the owner button, fades only left and right, and pushes the face toward cyan while pressed (Web SkitChoiceList.module.css .choice::before and .choice:active::before) */
.game-panel-surface--skit-choice {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    right: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    left: 0;
}

.game-panel-surface--skit-choice > .game-panel-surface__face {
    --game-panel-face: var(--gauge-track);
    --game-panel-edge-fade: var(--skit-choice-edge-fade);
    --game-panel-top-fade: 0px;
    --game-panel-bottom-fade: 0px;
}

/* 押下の状態は持ち主のボタンが持つので、ボタンの :active から板の面を引く（Theme.uss の .unity-button:active と同じ起点） */
/* The pressed state lives on the owner button, so the plate face keys off the button's :active (same anchor as Theme.uss .unity-button:active) */
.unity-button:active > .game-panel-surface--skit-choice > .game-panel-surface__face {
    --game-panel-face: var(--skit-choice-active-face);
}
```


```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Choices/SkitChoiceMarker.cs
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Theme;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Choices
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 選択肢の両端の菱形（Web ChoiceMarkerIcon。viewBox 12 の外側と内側の 2 つの菱形を線で描く）
    /// The diamond at each end of a choice (Web ChoiceMarkerIcon; strokes the outer and inner diamonds of a viewBox-12 icon)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitChoiceMarker : VisualElement
    {
        private const float ViewBoxSize = 12f;

        private Color _color;
        private float _stroke;
        private bool _hasTokens;

        public SkitChoiceMarker(string positionClassName)
        {
            AddToClassList("skit-choice__marker");
            AddToClassList(positionClassName);
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            generateVisualContent += Draw;
            RegisterCallback<CustomStyleResolvedEvent>(_ => OnCustomStyleResolved());
        }

        // ホバーで色が変わると customStyle が解き直されるので、そのたびに描き直す
        // Hover changes re-resolve the custom style, so redraw each time
        private void OnCustomStyleResolved()
        {
            var hasColor = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--skit-choice-marker-color", out _color);
            var hasStroke = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadNumber(customStyle, "--skit-choice-marker-stroke", out _stroke);
            _hasTokens = hasColor && hasStroke;
            MarkDirtyRepaint();
        }

        private void Draw(MeshGenerationContext context)
        {
            // silent-ok: トークンが読めない間は描かない / UssCustomStyleReader が読めなかったトークン名を LogError で出す
            if (!_hasTokens) return;

            // M6 0.5 L11.5 6 L6 11.5 L0.5 6 Z と M6 3.5 L8.5 6 L6 8.5 L3.5 6 Z
            // M6 0.5 L11.5 6 L6 11.5 L0.5 6 Z and M6 3.5 L8.5 6 L6 8.5 L3.5 6 Z
            var scale = contentRect.width / ViewBoxSize;
            var painter = context.painter2D;
            painter.lineWidth = _stroke * scale;
            painter.lineJoin = LineJoin.Miter;
            painter.strokeColor = _color;
            StrokeDiamond(5.5f);
            StrokeDiamond(2.5f);

            #region Internal

            void StrokeDiamond(float halfSize)
            {
                painter.BeginPath();
                painter.MoveTo(new Vector2(6f, 6f - halfSize) * scale);
                painter.LineTo(new Vector2(6f + halfSize, 6f) * scale);
                painter.LineTo(new Vector2(6f, 6f + halfSize) * scale);
                painter.LineTo(new Vector2(6f - halfSize, 6f) * scale);
                painter.ClosePath();
                painter.Stroke();
            }

            #endregion
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Choices/SkitChoiceButton.cs
using System;
using Client.Ui.Components.Panels;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Text;
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using UniRx;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Choices
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 選択肢の板 1 枚。面は左右だけ水平フェード、上下の線と菱形は内側に全不透明で載せ、文は中央に 1 行で置く（Web .choice）
    /// One choice plate; the face fades only at its left and right ends, the rules and diamonds sit inset at full opacity, and the label is one centered line (Web .choice)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitChoiceButton : Button
    {
        private readonly Subject<Unit> _onClicked = new();

        public IObservable<Unit> OnClicked => _onClicked;

        public SkitChoiceButton(SkitChoiceItem item, bool lead)
        {
            name = $"skit-choice-{item.ChoiceId}";
            focusable = false;
            text = string.Empty;
            AddToClassList("skit-choice");
            EnableInClassList("skit-choice--lead", lead);

            // 面は部品の種別 SkitChoice（左右だけの水平フェードと押下の面）。入力はこのボタンが受けるので板と本文の箱は拾わせない（D-37）
            // The face is the SkitChoice kind (side-only fade and pressed face); this button takes input, so neither the plate nor its body box is pickable (D-37)
            var plate = new GamePanelSurface($"skit-choice-{item.ChoiceId}-plate", GamePanelSurfaceKind.SkitChoice);
            plate.pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            plate.contentContainer.pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            Add(plate);
            Add(CreateDecor("skit-choice__rule"));
            Add(new SkitChoiceMarker("skit-choice__marker--start"));
            Add(new SkitChoiceMarker("skit-choice__marker--end"));

            // 文は両端の菱形を避けた箱の中央に置き、はみ出しは省略記号にする
            // The label centers in a box that clears the diamonds, with an ellipsis on overflow
            var labelBox = CreateDecor("skit-choice__label-box");
            var label = new LocalizedLabel();
            label.AddToClassList("skit-choice__label");
            label.pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            label.SetText(UiText.FromVerbatim(SkitRichText.Literal(item.Label)));
            labelBox.Add(label);
            Add(labelBox);

            clicked += () => _onClicked.OnNext(Unit.Default);
        }

        private static VisualElement CreateDecor(string className)
        {
            var element = new VisualElement { pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore };
            element.AddToClassList(className);
            return element;
        }
    }
}
```

（`SkitRichText` は Task 8 で作る。この Task のコンパイルを通すため、Task 8 Step 1 の `SkitRichText.cs` を先にこの Step で作ってよい。その場合 Task 8 では作り直さない）

- [ ] **Step 3: 選択肢の列を書く**

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Choices/SkitChoiceList.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;
using Client.Ui.State.Toast;
using UniRx;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Choices
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 会話窓の上・右端に下から積む選択肢の列（Web SkitChoiceList）。同じ選択肢の並びが続く間は板を作り直さない
    /// Choices stacked upward just above the window at its right edge (Web SkitChoiceList); plates are not rebuilt while the same choices continue
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitChoiceList : VisualElement
    {
        private readonly ISkitCommands _commands;
        private readonly UiCommandResultToaster _toaster;
        private readonly List<SkitChoiceButton> _buttons = new();
        private IReadOnlyList<SkitChoiceItem> _shownChoices = new List<SkitChoiceItem>();
        private SkitViewModel _model = SkitViewModel.Empty;
        private bool _interactive = true;

        public SkitChoiceList(ISkitCommands commands, UiCommandResultToaster toaster)
        {
            _commands = commands;
            _toaster = toaster;
            AddToClassList("skit-choices");
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            style.display = DisplayStyle.None;
        }

        public void Render(SkitViewModel model)
        {
            _model = model;
            if (!SameChoices(model.Choices)) Rebuild(model.Choices);
            style.display = model.Choices.Count > 0 ? DisplayStyle.Flex : DisplayStyle.None;
        }

        public void SetInteractive(bool interactive)
        {
            _interactive = interactive;
            foreach (var button in _buttons) button.pickingMode = interactive ? PickingMode.Position : PickingMode.Ignore;
        }

        private void Rebuild(IReadOnlyList<SkitChoiceItem> choices)
        {
            // 押したら押した時点の session と版で選ぶ
            // A press selects with the session and revision at that moment
            Clear();
            _buttons.Clear();
            for (var i = 0; i < choices.Count; i++)
            {
                var choiceId = choices[i].ChoiceId;
                var button = new SkitChoiceButton(choices[i], i == 0);
                button.pickingMode = _interactive ? PickingMode.Position : PickingMode.Ignore;
                button.OnClicked.Subscribe(_ => _toaster.Report("skit.select", _commands.Select(_model.SessionId, _model.SceneRevision, choiceId)));
                _buttons.Add(button);
                Add(button);
            }
            _shownChoices = choices;
        }

        private bool SameChoices(IReadOnlyList<SkitChoiceItem> choices)
        {
            if (choices.Count != _shownChoices.Count) return false;
            for (var i = 0; i < choices.Count; i++)
            {
                if (choices[i].ChoiceId != _shownChoices[i].ChoiceId || choices[i].Label != _shownChoices[i].Label) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
}
```

```css
/* S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Choices/SkitChoices.uss */
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/features/skit/controls/SkitChoiceList.module.css の .choices・.choice・.marker・.choiceLabel */
/* 会話窓の上・右端に下から積む固定寸法の板。面は左右だけフェードし、上下の線と菱形はホバーでシアン、押下で面をシアン寄りにする */
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/features/skit/controls/SkitChoiceList.module.css .choices, .choice, .marker and .choiceLabel */
/* Fixed-size plates stacked upward above the window at its right edge; the face fades at the sides only, rules and diamonds turn cyan on hover, and pressing pushes the face toward cyan */

.skit-choices {
    position: absolute;
    right: var(--skit-choices-right-inset);
    bottom: var(--skit-window-height);
    margin-bottom: var(--skit-choices-window-gap);
    flex-direction: column-reverse;
    align-items: flex-end;
}

/* .unity-button の既定（1 クラス）へ 2 クラスで勝ち、面・枠・余白を消す */
/* Two classes beat the .unity-button default (one class) and clear its face, border and padding */
.skit-choices .skit-choice {
    width: var(--skit-choice-width);
    height: var(--skit-choice-height);
    margin: 0;
    margin-bottom: var(--skit-choice-gap);
    padding: 0;
    border-width: 0;
    border-radius: 0;
    background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);
    color: var(--text-high-contrast);
    font-size: var(--skit-choice-font-size);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice--lead {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* 板の面（左右のフェード・押下の面）は部品 GamePanelSurface の種別 SkitChoice が持つ（D-37） */
/* The plate face (side fades and pressed face) belongs to the GamePanelSurface SkitChoice kind (D-37) */

/* 上下の線は板端でなく内側に引き、フェード帯の上も全不透明で描く */
/* The rules run inset from the plate edge and stay fully opaque over the fade band */
.skit-choices .skit-choice__rule {
    position: absolute;
    top: var(--skit-choice-rule-vertical-inset);
    right: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
    bottom: var(--skit-choice-rule-vertical-inset);
    left: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
    border-top-width: var(--skit-choice-rule-thickness);
    border-bottom-width: var(--skit-choice-rule-thickness);
    border-top-color: var(--bevel-c1);
    border-bottom-color: var(--bevel-c1);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice:hover .skit-choice__rule {
    border-top-color: var(--select-cyan);
    border-bottom-color: var(--select-cyan);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice__marker {
    position: absolute;
    top: 50%;
    width: var(--skit-choice-marker-size);
    height: var(--skit-choice-marker-size);
    translate: 0 -50%;
    --skit-choice-marker-color: var(--bevel-c2);
    --skit-choice-marker-stroke: var(--skit-choice-marker-stroke-width);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice:hover .skit-choice__marker {
    --skit-choice-marker-color: var(--select-cyan);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice__marker--start {
    left: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice__marker--end {
    right: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
}

/* 文の箱は線と同じ内側から菱形の幅だけさらに避ける（Web の max-width: calc(100% - 2 * (inset + marker))） */
/* The label box starts at the rules' inset and clears the diamond width too (Web max-width: calc(100% - 2 * (inset + marker))) */
.skit-choices .skit-choice__label-box {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    right: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
    bottom: 0;
    left: var(--skit-choice-rule-inset);
    padding-left: var(--skit-choice-marker-size);
    padding-right: var(--skit-choice-marker-size);
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
}

.skit-choices .skit-choice__label {
    max-width: 100%;
    overflow: hidden;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    -unity-text-align: middle-center;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 字の並べ方を書き、透明な残りで配置が止まるかを先に実測する（A-82 の測り分け）**

`SkitRichText.cs`（Task 7 Step 2 で先に作っていれば飛ばす）:

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Window/SkitRichText.cs
using Client.Ui.State.Skit.Logic;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window
{
    /// <summary>
    /// スキットの文を Label のリッチテキストへ組む。文の中の山括弧はタグにせず、字を 1 つも削らずに字のまま出す（Web は文字列をそのまま描く）
    /// Builds skit text into Label rich text; angle brackets inside the text stay literal and no character is dropped (the Web draws the string as is)
    /// </summary>
    public static class SkitRichText
    {
        // 残りの字を透明にする開きタグ。Task 8 Step 1 の実測で決める（D-10）
        // Opening tag that turns the remaining characters transparent; settled by the Task 8 Step 1 measurement (D-10)
        public const string HiddenTailOpenTag = "<alpha=#00>";
        // 「<」の直後に挟む幅 0 の字。語結合子は改行の機会も作らない。Task 8 Step 1 の実測で決める（D-48）
        // Zero-width character inserted right after '<'; a word joiner adds no line-break opportunity; settled by the Task 8 Step 1 measurement (D-48)
        private const string TagBreaker = "\u2060";
        private const string BackgroundSeparator = " : ";

        public static string Literal(string text)
        {
            // どの「<」もタグの始まりにならないよう、直後に幅 0 の字を挟む。noparse と違い閉じタグの字面も削らずに済む
            // Insert a zero-width character after every '<' so none starts a tag; unlike noparse, a literal closing tag needs no removal
            return text.Replace("<", "<" + TagBreaker);
        }

        // 出した字の後ろに残りの字を透明で続け、折り返しと行の位置を全文と同じに保つ（A-82）
        // Follow the shown characters with the rest in transparent, keeping wrapping and line positions identical to the full text (A-82)
        public static string TypewriterBody(string body, int visibleCodePoints)
        {
            var shownLength = SkitTypewriterRules.PrefixLengthOfCodePoints(body, visibleCodePoints);
            if (shownLength >= body.Length) return Literal(body);
            return Literal(body.Substring(0, shownLength)) + HiddenTailOpenTag + Literal(body.Substring(shownLength));
        }

        // 背景の 1 行は Web と同じく切り出す。中央寄せの 1 行なので透明な残りを足すと位置が Web とずれる（D-9）
        // The background line slices like the Web; it is a centered single line, so a transparent tail would shift it from the Web (D-9)
        public static string BackgroundLine(string speaker, string body, int visibleCodePoints)
        {
            var visibleBody = body.Substring(0, SkitTypewriterRules.PrefixLengthOfCodePoints(body, visibleCodePoints));
            return Literal(speaker.Length == 0 ? visibleBody : speaker + BackgroundSeparator + visibleBody);
        }
    }
}
```

測るテスト（View を使わず、本文と同じ USS のクラスを付けた `LocalizedLabel` だけで測る）:

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/SkitTypewriterLayoutTest.cs
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Text;
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit.Logic;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 打ち出し中も本文の箱の高さが全文と同じであること（残りを透明にして配置を止める。A-82）と、文の山括弧・閉じタグの字面が字を落とさず出ることを確かめる（L1）
    /// Verifies the body box keeps the full text's height while typing (the rest turns transparent to freeze the layout; A-82) and that angle brackets and closing-tag literals show without dropping characters (L1)
    /// </summary>
    public class SkitTypewriterLayoutTest : ScreenTestHost
    {
        private const string LongBody = "The conveyor is jammed again. Check the splitter near the furnace,\nthen bring three iron plates back to the hub before the night shift starts.";
        private const string TagLikeBody = "A</noparse>B<b>C</b><alpha=#00>D<";

        [Test]
        public void 打ち出し中も本文の箱の高さと幅が全文と変わらない()
        {
            var full = MountBody(SkitRichText.TypewriterBody(LongBody, SkitTypewriterRules.CountCodePoints(LongBody)));
            var typing = MountBody(SkitRichText.TypewriterBody(LongBody, 12));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            Assert.Greater(full.layout.height, 40f, "全文が 2 行以上に折り返していない（測り方の前提）");
            Assert.AreEqual(full.layout.height, typing.layout.height, 0.5f, $"打ち出し中の高さが変わる（{SkitRichText.HiddenTailOpenTag}）");
            Assert.AreEqual(full.layout.width, typing.layout.width, 0.5f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 文の山括弧はタグにせず字のまま出す()
        {
            var literal = MountShrunkBody(SkitRichText.Literal("<b>M</b>"));
            var plain = MountShrunkBody(SkitRichText.Literal("M"));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            Assert.Greater(literal.contentRect.width, plain.contentRect.width * 3f, "<b> がタグとして解釈された");
        }

        [Test]
        public void 閉じタグの字面を含む文も全文と打ち出しのどの区切りでも字を落とさない()
        {
            // Web は文字列をそのまま描くので、リッチテキスト無しで描いた生の文の幅を正とする
            // The Web draws the string as is, so the raw text drawn without rich text is the reference width
            var reference = MountShrunkRaw(TagLikeBody);
            var count = SkitTypewriterRules.CountCodePoints(TagLikeBody);
            var splits = new LocalizedLabel[count + 1];
            for (var visible = 0; visible <= count; visible++) splits[visible] = MountShrunkBody(SkitRichText.TypewriterBody(TagLikeBody, visible));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            // 透明な残りも幅を持つので、どの区切りも全文と同じ幅。字が消えれば縮み、タグとして読まれれば幅が変わる
            // The transparent tail keeps its width, so every split matches the full width; a dropped character shrinks it and a parsed tag changes it
            Assert.Greater(reference.contentRect.width, 0f, "生の文が描かれていない（測り方の前提）");
            for (var visible = 0; visible <= count; visible++)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual(reference.contentRect.width, splits[visible].contentRect.width, 0.5f, $"先頭 {visible} 字で区切ると幅が生の文と違う");
            }
        }

        private LocalizedLabel MountBody(string richText)
        {
            // 窓の本文と同じクラスと幅（実画面 1280 − 左 168 − 右 111 の内側）で測る
            // Measure with the window body's class and width (inside 1280 minus 168 left and 111 right)
            var label = new LocalizedLabel();
            label.style.width = 1280f;
            label.SetText(UiText.FromVerbatim(richText));
            MountWithBodyClass(label);
            return label;
        }

        // 幅を字の並びに合わせる。固定幅だとどの文も同じ幅になり比べられない
        // Size the width to the glyph run; a fixed width would make every text equally wide and uncomparable
        private LocalizedLabel MountShrunkBody(string richText)
        {
            var label = new LocalizedLabel();
            label.style.position = Position.Absolute;
            label.SetText(UiText.FromVerbatim(richText));
            MountWithBodyClass(label);
            return label;
        }

        private Label MountShrunkRaw(string text)
        {
            var label = new Label(text) { enableRichText = false };
            label.style.position = Position.Absolute;
            MountWithBodyClass(label);
            return label;
        }

        private void MountWithBodyClass(TextElement label)
        {
            // 窓の本文と同じクラス（字の大きさ・余白・行高）を当てる。比べるのは余白を除いた contentRect
            // Apply the window body's class (size, padding, line height); comparisons use contentRect without the padding
            label.AddToClassList("skit-window__body");
            var host = new VisualElement();
            host.AddToClassList("skit-screen");
            host.Add(label);
            Mount(host);
        }
    }
}
```

（このテストは Step 5 の `Skit.uss` の `.skit-window__body` の字の大きさ・余白・行高に依る。Step 5 の USS を書いてから流す）

Step 5 の USS を書いた後で流す:

Run: `uloop compile --project-path ./moorestech_client` → `uloop run-tests --project-path ./moorestech_client --filter-type class --filter-value "Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit.SkitTypewriterLayoutTest"`

結果で次のように分ける（D-10）。分けた結果と測った高さは、判断記録 D-10 の「計測」に書く。

1. 3 件とも PASS: `<alpha=#00>` と語結合子のまま進む。配置固定は完了条件どおりなので止めない。done.md の「要裁定」に「止めずに進めた。Web と違う見た目（打ち出し中に行末の語が跳ばない）を許すかの確認」と 1 行書き、LX-51 に記帳する（D-42）
2. 1 件目だけ FAIL: `HiddenTailOpenTag` を `"<color=#00000000>"` に替えて流し直す。PASS ならそれで進む（done.md の要裁定の 1 行と LX-51 は 1 と同じ）。3 件目も同じ開きタグで流し直す
3. それでも 1 件目が FAIL: UITK のリッチテキストで透明な残りは配置を保てない。完了条件の「配置固定」を満たせないので、ここだけは止める（D-42）。done.md の「要裁定」に「透明な残りでは配置を保てない（測った高さ）。Web と同じ切り出しにしてよいか」と書き、案として `TypewriterBody` を `Literal(body.Substring(0, shownLength))`（切り出し。Web と同じ）に替え、1 件目のテストを消し、3 件目の比べる相手を全文の生の文から「先頭 visible 字の生の文」に替える形を添える。LX-51 は使わない
4. 2 件目か 3 件目が FAIL（語結合子 U+2060 が幅を持って描かれる、または山括弧がタグとして読まれて字が消える）: `TagBreaker` をゼロ幅空白 `"\u200B"` に替えて流し直す。PASS ならそれで進み、ゼロ幅空白は `<` の後ろで改行を許す（Web は許さない）差を done.md に書く。それでも FAIL なら止め、done.md の「要裁定」に「文の山括弧と閉じタグの字面を、字を削らずに出す形が UITK に無い（測った幅）」と書く。字を削る形やタグとして読ませる形へは落とさない（D-48）

```csharp
// S/Client.Tests/Ui/Screens/Skit/SkitScreenViewTest.cs
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Text;
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit;
using Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Screens.Skit
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 会話窓の打ち出し・全文表示・送り・選択と、背景の 1 行・UI 非表示・演出中・暗転の出し分けを確かめる（L1。Web skit.spec・phaseC4.spec）
    /// Verifies typing, reveal, advance and select on the window, and how the background line, hidden UI, staging and transition show (L1; Web skit.spec and phaseC4.spec)
    /// </summary>
    public class SkitScreenViewTest : ScreenTestHost
    {
        private const string SecondBody = "Second message";

        private RecordingSkitCommands _commands;
        private SkitScreenView _view;

        [SetUp]
        public void MountScreen()
        {
            _commands = new RecordingSkitCommands();
            _view = Mount(new SkitScreenView(_commands, Toaster));
        }

        /// <web-test file="e2e/tests/skit.spec.ts">blocking skit reveals, advances, and selects by choiceId</web-test>
        [Test]
        public void 打ち出し中のクリックは全文を出し次のクリックで送り選択肢はchoiceIdで選ぶ()
        {
            // 最初の描画は復元なので全文、次の本文から打ち出す
            // The first draw is a restore and shows everything; the next body is typed
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Text(SkitTestData.SessionId, 1, "Blocking message", SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Text(SkitTestData.SessionId, 2, SecondBody, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdateMs(120);
            Assert.AreNotEqual(Expected(SecondBody, 14), BodyText(), "打ち出しが始まっていない");

            simulate.Click(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "blocking-skit"));
            Assert.AreEqual(Expected(SecondBody, 14), BodyText());
            Assert.AreEqual(0, _commands.Issued.Count, "全文表示のクリックで送った");

            simulate.Click(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "blocking-skit"));
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Advance, SkitTestData.SessionId, 2, null, false), _commands.Issued[0]);

            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Choices(3, SkitTestData.ChoiceIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            simulate.Click(Find<Button>(_view, "skit-choice-route-b"));
            Assert.AreEqual(new SkitCommandRecord(SkitCommandKind.Select, SkitTestData.SessionId, 3, "route-b", false), _commands.Issued[1]);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 送りの印は全文が出て送りが許可されたときだけ出る()
        {
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Text(SkitTestData.SessionId, 1, "Blocking message", SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Text(SkitTestData.SessionId, 2, SecondBody, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdateMs(60);
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-advance-marker")));

            simulate.FrameUpdateMs(2000);
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-advance-marker")));

            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Text(SkitTestData.SessionId, 3, SecondBody, new[] { "set-auto" }));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-advance-marker")));
        }

        /// <web-test file="e2e/tests/phaseC4.spec.ts">background skit snapshot renders speaker and body</web-test>
        [Test]
        public void 背景スキットの提示は話者と本文を1行で出し窓は出さない()
        {
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Background(1, "Moore", "Background message"));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            var line = Find<Label>(_view, "background-skit");
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(line));
            Assert.AreEqual(SkitRichText.Literal("Moore : Background message"), line.text);
            Assert.AreEqual(PickingMode.Ignore, line.pickingMode);
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-window")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-tool-auto")));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 背景の空本文と提示なしでは何も出さない()
        {
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Background(1, "Moore", ""));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<Label>(_view, "background-skit")));

            _view.Render(SkitTestData.None(2));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-window")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-tool-auto")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-show-ui")));
        }

        [Test]
        public void UI非表示の間は復帰ボタンだけを出す()
        {
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Controls(1, true, false, false, false, true, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-window")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-tool-auto")));
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-show-ui")));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 演出で本文の窓が閉じている間はツールだけを出す()
        {
            _view.Render(SkitTestData.Controls(1, false, false, false, false, false, SkitTestData.TextIntents));
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-window")));
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-tool-auto")));
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(Find<VisualElement>(_view, "skit-show-ui")));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 暗転は実画面いっぱいの黒を即座に出し入力を奪わない()
        {
            var transition = Mount(new SkitTransitionView());
            Assert.IsFalse(IsShown(transition));

            transition.SetShown(true);
            Assert.IsTrue(IsShown(transition));
            Assert.AreEqual(new Rect(0, 0, 1280, 720), transition.worldBound);
            Assert.AreEqual(1f, transition.resolvedStyle.backgroundColor.a, 0.001f);
            Assert.AreEqual(PickingMode.Ignore, transition.pickingMode);
        }

        private string BodyText()
        {
            return Find<LocalizedLabel>(_view, "skit-body").text;
        }

        // 本文の Label は行高のタグを前置するので、末尾の一致で比べる
        // The body Label prefixes a line-height tag, so compare by suffix
        private string Expected(string body, int visibleCodePoints)
        {
            var expected = SkitRichText.TypewriterBody(body, visibleCodePoints);
            var actual = BodyText();
            return actual.EndsWith(expected) ? actual : expected;
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: タイプライタを書く**

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Window/SkitTypewriter.cs
using System;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Motion.Playback;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit.Logic;
using UniRx;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 本文の何字目まで出したかを持ち、経過時間で 1 字ずつ増やす（Web SkitPresentation の visibleCount と setInterval）。面をまたいで同じ進みを使う
    /// Holds how many characters of the body are out and adds one per interval (Web SkitPresentation visibleCount and setInterval); the same progress spans surfaces
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitTypewriter
    {
        private readonly VisualElement _clockHost;
        private readonly Subject<Unit> _onVisibleCountChanged = new();
        private UiMotionHandle _handle;
        private string _sessionId = string.Empty;
        private string _body = string.Empty;
        private bool _revealsByTypewriter;
        private int _intervalMs;
        private bool _presenting;

        public int VisibleCount { get; private set; }
        public int TotalCount { get; private set; }
        public IObservable<Unit> OnVisibleCountChanged => _onVisibleCountChanged;

        public SkitTypewriter(VisualElement clockHost)
        {
            _clockHost = clockHost;
        }

        // Web の useEffect の依存（session・本文・表示方式・間隔）が変わったときだけ頭から打ち直す
        // Restart from the head only when the Web useEffect dependencies (session, body, mode, interval) change
        public void Follow(SkitViewModel model, bool restoringSnapshot)
        {
            if (!restoringSnapshot && !ChangedFrom(model)) return;
            Remember(model);
            StopTyping();
            TotalCount = SkitTypewriterRules.CountCodePoints(model.Body);

            // 提示なしは 0、復元と即時表示は全文、それ以外は 0 から打つ
            // No presentation means zero, restore and instant mean everything, otherwise type from zero
            if (!model.IsPresenting)
            {
                SetVisibleCount(0);
                return;
            }
            if (SkitTypewriterRules.ShouldRevealImmediately(restoringSnapshot, model.RevealsByTypewriter, model.RevealIntervalMs))
            {
                SetVisibleCount(TotalCount);
                return;
            }
            SetVisibleCount(0);
            _handle = UiMotionPlayer.Play(_clockHost, new SkitTypewriterAnimation(this, model.RevealIntervalMs, TotalCount));
        }

        public void RevealAll()
        {
            StopTyping();
            SetVisibleCount(TotalCount);
        }

        internal void SetVisibleCount(int count)
        {
            if (count == VisibleCount) return;
            VisibleCount = count;
            _onVisibleCountChanged.OnNext(Unit.Default);
        }

        private bool ChangedFrom(SkitViewModel model)
        {
            return model.SessionId != _sessionId || model.Body != _body || model.RevealsByTypewriter != _revealsByTypewriter || model.RevealIntervalMs != _intervalMs || model.IsPresenting != _presenting;
        }

        private void Remember(SkitViewModel model)
        {
            _sessionId = model.SessionId;
            _body = model.Body;
            _revealsByTypewriter = model.RevealsByTypewriter;
            _intervalMs = model.RevealIntervalMs;
            _presenting = model.IsPresenting;
        }

        private void StopTyping()
        {
            _handle?.Cancel();
            _handle = null;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
// S/Client.Ui/Screens/Skit/Parts/Window/SkitTypewriterAnimation.cs
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Motion.Core;
using Client.Ui.State.Skit.Logic;

namespace Client.Ui.Screens.Skit.Parts.Window
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 開始からの経過時間で出す字数を書く動き。全文が出たら終わる
    /// A motion that writes the shown character count from the elapsed time since start; finishes once everything is out
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SkitTypewriterAnimation : IUiAnimation
    {
        private readonly SkitTypewriter _typewriter;
        private readonly int _intervalMs;
        private readonly int _totalCount;

        public SkitTypewriterAnimation(SkitTypewriter typewriter, int intervalMs, int totalCount)
        {
            _typewriter = typewriter;
            _intervalMs = intervalMs;
            _totalCount = totalCount;
        }

        public void Begin()
        {
            _typewriter.SetVisibleCount(0);
        }

        public bool Advance(float elapsedMilliseconds)
        {
            var count = SkitTypewriterRules.RevealCountAt(elapsedMilliseconds, _intervalMs, _totalCount);
            _typewriter.SetVisibleCount(count);
            return count >= _totalCount;
        }
    }
}
```
